      # Vertical knockback force
      height-force: 1.0
  
      # Horizontal knockback force
      radius-force: 1.5
  
      # Extra damage on top of the distance-based damage
      damage: 0.5
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Messages
  #  Normal PvP kill message
  #  %killer% = killer name (GREEN)
  #  %loser%  = victim name (RED)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  kill: '&a%killer% &7killed &c%loser%'
  
  Title-Suffix: '  &7Kill'
  SubTitle-kill: ' &e+1 Kill'
  join-message: ' &e%player%  &7joined the game  &8( &e%online% &7/ &e100 &8)'
  quit-message: ' &e%player%  &7left the game  &8( &e%online% &7/ &e100 &8)'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Infinite Food & Blocks
  #
  #  anti-hunger: player never gets hungry
  #  ⚠️ NOTE: With this enabled, food does NOT heal HP.
  #     Only Golden Apples / Potions can heal HP.
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  infinite:
    food: true
    blocks: true
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Kill Heal (BedWars-style)
  #  When a player kills someone, they receive:
  #    - Full HP (or configurable amount)
  #    - Fire reset
  #    - Fall distance reset
  #    - Optional Absorption hearts
  #
  #  ⚠️ Food is NOT refilled here — that's handled by
  #     infinite.food above.
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  kill-heal:
    # Enable/disable the heal-on-kill
    enabled: true
  
    # If true, killer's HP is set to max
    # If false, `amount` is added instead
    full-heal: true
  
    # Only used when full-heal: false
    # Example: 6.0 = 3 hearts
    amount: 6.0
  
    # Absorption hearts (golden bonus hearts)
    # Only works on 1.6+
    absorption:
      enabled: false
      # 0 = 2 hearts, 1 = 4 hearts, 2 = 6 hearts, etc.
      level: 1
      # Duration in seconds
      duration: 5
  
  # ==========================================================
  #  ⚠️  OWN HealthBar — DISABLED
  # ==========================================================
  #  This used to show YOUR OWN HP above the hotbar
  #  (e.g. "❤ 20/20"). It is now fully disabled because
  #  the ENEMY health bar (below) handles all PvP health display.
  #
  #  → To re-enable your own HP bar, set enabled: true
  #    AND make sure HealthBarManager is instantiated in BuildFFA.java
  # ==========================================================
  healthbar:
    # DISABLED by default — own HP bar no longer shown
    enabled: false
  
    # Update interval in ticks (20 = 1 second)
    # 5 = smooth, 10 = medium, 20 = every second
    update-interval: 5
  
    # Mode: NUMERIC | HEARTS | PERCENT
    mode: NUMERIC
  
    # Format for NUMERIC and PERCENT modes
    #   %current%  ->  current HP
    #   %max%      ->  max HP
    #   %percent%  ->  HP percent (0-100)
    #   %player%   ->  player name
    format: '&c❤ &f%current%&7/&f%max%'
  
    # Colors for HEARTS mode
    filled-color: '&c'
    empty-color: '&7'
    heart-char: '❤'
  
  # ==========================================================
  #  ✅ Enemy HealthBar (ActionBar)
  # ==========================================================
  #  Shows the HP of the player you LAST HIT, above your hotbar.
  #  Perfect for PvP — you see enemy HP without cluttering the screen.
  #
  #  HOW IT WORKS:
  #    - You hit someone → their HP appears in your action bar
  #    - You hit again     → HP refreshes
  #    - You stop hitting → bar disappears after `timeout-seconds`
  #
  #  PLACEHOLDERS:
  #    %name%  ->  enemy player name
  #    %hp%    ->  enemy current HP
  #    %max%   ->  enemy max HP
  #
  #  FORMAT EXAMPLES:
  #    '&c[%name%] &8-- &f[%hp%&7/&f%max%&f]'   →  [Notch] -- [18/20]
  #    '&c%name% &8» &f%hp%&7/&f%max%'           →  Notch » 18/20
  #    '&e❤ &f%name% &7- &c%hp%&7/&c%max%'       →  ❤ Notch - 18/20
  #    '&8[&c%name%&8] &f%hp%&7/&f%max%'         →  [Notch] 18/20
  # ==========================================================
  enemy-healthbar:
    # Enable/disable the enemy HP action bar
    enabled: true
  
    # Update interval in ticks (20 ticks = 1 second)
    # 5 = smooth (recommended), 10 = medium
    update-interval: 5
  
    # After this many seconds without hitting anyone,
    # the enemy HP bar disappears automatically.
    timeout-seconds: 5
  
    # Format string — see examples above
    format: '&c[%name%] &8-- &f[%hp%&7/&f%max%&f]'
  
  # ==========================================================
  #  Nametag HP Display (BELOW player's name)
  # ==========================================================
  #  Shows live HP below each player's name (like BedWars).
  #
  #  ✅ Uses HEALTH criteria → client auto-renders "20 ❤"
  #  ✅ Uses pure NMS packets (safe on 1.8.8)
  #  ✅ No prefix/suffix → no "22 > 16" disconnect
  #
  #  IMPORTANT:
  #    On 1.8.8 the client ALWAYS renders the format as:
  #         {display-name} {score}
  #    The heart ❤ AFTER the number is added automatically
  #    by the vanilla client when the objective uses the
  #    "health" criteria and display-name is EMPTY.
  #
  #  display-name: the text shown BEFORE the HP number
  #    ''            → "20 ❤"   ✅ (exactly like the image)
  #    '&c❤'         → "❤ 20"
  #    '&cHP'        → "HP 20"
  #    '&7[&c❤&7]'   → "[❤] 20"
  # ==========================================================
  nametag:
    enabled: true
    update-interval: 10
    # خالی = فقط عدد + قلب خودکار وانیلا → "20 ❤"
    display-name: ''
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Permissions
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  permissions:
    ypvp: buildffa.ypvp
    ypvp-bypass: buildffa.ypvp.bypass
    ypearl: buildffa.ypearl
    ypearl-bypass: buildffa.ypearl.bypass
    fireball-speed: buildffa.fireball.speed
    kiteditor: buildffa.kiteditor
    setvoid: buildffa.setvoid
    sethighlimit: buildffa.sethighlimit
    setspawn: buildffa.setspawn
    buildmode: buildffa.buildmode
    reload: buildffa.reload
    scoreboard-toggle: buildffa.scoreboard.toggle
    highlimit-bypass: buildffa.highlimit.bypass
    resetstats: buildffa.resetstats
  
  messages:
    no-permission: '&cYou do not have permission to do this.'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Kill Screen (Title/Subtitle on kill)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  kill-screen:
    enable-title: true
    title: '&e+%killcount% &7Kill'
    subtitle: '&e+1 Kill'
    enable-chat-fallback: false
    chat-message: '&a+1 Kill!'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Sounds
  #  Sound effects for kills, deaths, joins
  #  NOTE: No sound on hits (too spammy)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  sounds:
    enabled: true
    kill: true
    death: true
    join: true
    chat: false
    volume: 1.0
    pitch: 1.0
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Spawn
  #  Set with /buildffa setspawn
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  spawn:
    world: world
    x: -83.61916298668011
    y: 69.46495898148244
    z: 90.46339979325055
    yaw: 90.342545
    pitch: 5.961797
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Leaderboard Refresh (used by hologram countdown)
  #  %buildffa_next_update% counts down from this value
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  leaderboard-refresh:
    interval-seconds: 600
            ├── kit-setting.yml
  # ==========================================================
  #  BuildFFA - Server Default Kit Settings
  #  File: kit-setting.yml
  #
  #  This file defines the DEFAULT kit that every player gets
  #  when they join / respawn / clear their inventory.
  #
  #  Player-specific kits (saved in SQLite via /buildffa kiteditor)
  #  OVERRIDE this default kit.
  #
  #  ENCHANTMENT NAMES (1.8.8):
  #    DAMAGE_ALL          Sharpness
  #    DAMAGE_UNDEAD       Smite
  #    DAMAGE_ARTHROPODS   Bane of Arthropods
  #    KNOCKBACK           Knockback
  #    FIRE_ASPECT         Fire Aspect
  #    LOOT_BONUS_MOBS     Looting
  #    DIG_SPEED           Efficiency
  #    SILK_TOUCH          Silk Touch
  #    DURABILITY          Unbreaking
  #    LOOT_BONUS_BLOCKS   Fortune
  #    ARROW_DAMAGE        Power
  #    ARROW_KNOCKBACK     Punch
  #    ARROW_FIRE          Flame
  #    ARROW_INFINITE      Infinity
  #    PROTECTION_ENVIRONMENTAL  Protection
  #    PROTECTION_EXPLOSIONS     Blast Protection
  #    PROTECTION_FALL           Feather Falling
  #    PROTECTION_FIRE           Fire Protection
  #    PROTECTION_PROJECTILE     Projectile Protection
  #    OXYGEN              Respiration
  #    WATER_WORKER        Aqua Affinity
  #    THORNS              Thorns
  #    DEPTH_STRIDER       Depth Strider
  #
  #  COLOR options (leather armor only):
  #    RED, BLUE, GREEN, YELLOW, AQUA, BLACK, WHITE, PURPLE, ORANGE,
  #    FUCHSIA, GRAY, SILVER, LIME, MAROON, NAVY, OLIVE, TEAL
  #    OR "r,g,b"   example:  "255,0,0"
  #    OR "#RRGGBB" example:  "#FF0000"
  #
  #  ATTACK-DAMAGE:
  #    Optional. Overrides the vanilla attack damage of the item.
  #    Example: attack-damage: 7.5
  #
  #  POTION EFFECTS (for POTION items):
  #    effects:
  #      - 'JUMP:1:4'      # JUMP, duration in seconds, amplifier (0=I, 4=V)
  #      - 'SPEED:180:1'   # SPEED, 180s, amplifier 1 (Speed II)
  # ==========================================================
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  ARMOR
  #  Helmet + Chestplate = Leather RED (Protection II, Unbreakable)
  #  Leggings + Boots    = Diamond    (Protection II, Unbreakable)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  armor:

            ├── plugin.yml
  name: BuildFFA
  version: 4.0
  main: org.vansama.buildffa.BuildFFA
  author: muvixo
  authors: [muvixo, VanSaMa]
  description: BuildFFA - Created by Muvixo, made by PixelValley
  api-version: 1.8
  softdepend: [PlaceholderAPI]
  
  commands:
    bffa:
      description: Main BuildFFA command
      usage: /bffa <help|kiteditor|setvoid|sethighlimit|setspawn|buildmode|ypvp|ypearl|fb-speed|sb|stats|top|deathleaderboard|killleaderboard|kdrleaderboard|streakleaderboard|leaderboards|resetstats|resetallleaderboards|forceksreward|creator|reload>
      aliases:
        - buildffa
      # ★ FIX: gate the whole command at the Bukkit level — anyone without
      # this permission can't even run it (also hides it from tab-complete).
      permission: buildffa.use
      permission-message: '&cYou do not have permission to use this command.'
  
  permissions:
    buildffa.*:
      description: Grants all BuildFFA permissions
      default: op
      children:
        buildffa.use: true
        buildffa.kiteditor: true
        buildffa.setvoid: true
        buildffa.sethighlimit: true
        buildffa.setspawn: true
        buildffa.reload: true
        buildffa.highlimit.bypass: true
        buildffa.ypvp: true
        buildffa.ypvp.bypass: true
        buildffa.ypearl: true
        buildffa.ypearl.bypass: true
        buildffa.fireball.speed: true
        buildffa.buildmode: true
        buildffa.scoreboard.toggle: true
        buildffa.resetstats: true
  
    # ★ FIX: new top-level permission. Only op / players explicitly
    # granted this (or buildffa.*) can see or run ANY /bffa subcommand.
    buildffa.use:
      description: Allows seeing and using the /bffa command at all
      default: op
  
    buildffa.kiteditor:
      description: Allows using the Kit Editor
      default: true
  
    buildffa.setvoid:
      description: Allows setting the void kill height
      default: op
  
    buildffa.sethighlimit:
      description: Allows setting the high build limit
      default: op
  
    buildffa.setspawn:
      description: Allows setting the respawn point
      default: op
  
    buildffa.reload:
      description: Allows reloading the config
      default: op
  
    buildffa.highlimit.bypass:
      description: Bypass the high-limit build restriction
      default: op
  
    buildffa.ypvp:
      description: Allows setting the YPvP Y level
      default: op
  
    buildffa.ypvp.bypass:
      description: Bypass the YPvP PvP restriction
      default: op
  
    buildffa.ypearl:
      description: Allows setting the YPearl Y level
      default: op
  
    buildffa.ypearl.bypass:
      description: Bypass the YPearl throw restriction
      default: op
  
    buildffa.fireball.speed:
      description: Allows changing the fireball speed
      default: op
  
    buildffa.buildmode:
      description: Allows toggling build mode (high-limit bypass)
      default: op
  
    buildffa.scoreboard.toggle:
      description: Allows toggling the scoreboard visibility
      default: true
  
    buildffa.resetstats:
      description: Allows resetting player stats and forcing killstreak rewards
      default: op

  # ==========================================================
  #  BuildFFA Scoreboard Configuration
  #  Version: 4.0  |  Compatible: 1.8.8
  #  Theme: Aqua / White / Dark-Aqua (No Bold)
  #  Created by Muvixo | Author: VanSaMa | Made by PixelValley
  # ==========================================================
  #
  #  This file is AUTO-MERGED on plugin update.
  #  New keys are added automatically.
  #  Your existing lines are NEVER overwritten.
  # ==========================================================
  
  enabled: true
  update-interval: 10
  join-delay: 5
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Title Animation
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  title:
    animated: true
    static: '&bBuildFFA'
    frames:
      - '&bB&fuildFFA'
      - '&bBu&fildFFA'
      - '&bBui&flldFFA'
      - '&bBuil&fdFFA'
      - '&bBuild&fFFA'
      - '&bBuildF&fFA'
      - '&bBuildFF&fA'
      - '&bBuildFFA'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Scoreboard Lines
  #
  #  ⚠️ 1.8.8 LIMIT: each line = prefix (16) + suffix (16) = max 32 chars TOTAL
  #     If a line is longer, it gets split. The split may cut off the end.
  #
  #  Internal placeholders (handled by ScoreboardManager):
  #    %player% %kills% %deaths% %kdr% %killstreak% %best_killstreak%
  #    %online% %max_online% %world% %ping% %health% %food% %y%
  #    %highlimit% %void%
  #
  #  PlaceholderAPI placeholders (handled by BuildFFAExpansion):
  #    %buildffa_kills% %buildffa_deaths% %buildffa_kdr%
  #    %buildffa_killstreak% %buildffa_best_killstreak%
  #    %buildffa_top_name_killstreak_1%
  #    %buildffa_top_value_killstreak_1%
  #    %buildffa_top_name_kills_1%
  #    %buildffa_top_value_kills_1%
  #    %buildffa_top_rank_kills%
  #    %buildffa_top_rank_kdr%
  #    %buildffa_next_update%
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  lines:
    - '&3&m----------------'
    - '&bPlayer &8» &f%player%'
    - '&bKills &8» &f%kills%'
    - '&bDeaths &8» &f%deaths%'
    - '&bKDR &8» &f%kdr%'
    - '&bStreak &8» &f%killstreak%'
    - '&bBest KS &8» &f%best_killstreak%'
    - '&3&m----------------'
    - '&bTop KS &8» &f%buildffa_top_name_killstreak_1%'
    - '&3&m----------------'
    - '&bOnline &8» &f%online%&7/&f%max_online%'
    - '&bPing &8» &f%ping%ms'
    - '&3&m----------------'
    - '&bPlay.MineStorm.iR'
    - '&3&m----------------'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Per-World Scoreboards
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  per-world:
    enabled: false
    worlds:
      world:
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bOVERWORLD'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bPlayer &8» &f%player%'
        - '&bKills &8» &f%kills%'
        - '&bDeaths &8» &f%deaths%'
        - '&bKDR &8» &f%kdr%'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bOnline &8» &f%online%'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bPlay.MineStorm.iR'
        - '&3&m----------------'
      world_nether:
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bNETHER'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bPlayer &8» &f%player%'
        - '&bKills &8» &f%kills%'
        - '&bDeaths &8» &f%deaths%'
        - '&bKDR &8» &f%kdr%'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bOnline &8» &f%online%'
        - '&3&m----------------'
        - '&bPlay.MineStorm.iR'
        - '&3&m----------------'
  
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  #  Toggle
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  allow-toggle: true
  toggle-permission: 'buildffa.scoreboard.toggle'
